HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1c960615d7c227dd6779","text":"1. **Ground made into capacity.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء ر ض/B001` is the lower ground opposite the sky, and fixed `ط ح و/B001` is spreading and extending. **Structural cue and role:** the clause answers the built-above of 91:5 with a spread-below and immediately supports the formed self of 91:7; the earth therefore functions as an intentionally enlarged field of action. **Abductive move:** vertical pairing and sequence are explicit; assigning usable capacity as the purpose of spreading is inferred. **Reading change:** earth is not passive matter but prepared room in which agency can occur.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000928/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_36768b5acf9323f6b21b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:6:1\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:6:3\",\"root_000928\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Göğün altında yayılan yer, canın eylemde bulunabileceği hazırlanmış ve genişletilmiş bir imkân alanı hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B002","root_000766/B009","root_000928/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5d1d5053e8eeba5e5a8b","text":"3. **Sprawling/flattening foreshadows punitive leveling.** Lexical evidence: ط ح و B006 \"sprawling against the ground\"; س و ي B002 \"straightness and sound completion\" and B009 \"wide or smooth open ground\"; د م د م B001 complete eradication in 91:14. Structural cue: طحىها spreads the earth early; فسوىها levels after punishment late. Assigned role: طحى's spreading can be benign creation, while later سوى becomes imposed leveling. Abductive move: I infer a reversal between creative flattening and punitive flattening; the packet supplies both spread-ground and final leveling roots. Reading change: the spread earth becomes an ominous template: what is made level for life can also be leveled over a destroyed community.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000745/B004","root_000928/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60a7beda85251f6b1b76","text":"1. **Earth as lower counterpart to the built cover.** Lexical evidence: ء ر ض B001 \"lower ground opposite the sky\"; ط ح و B001 \"spreading and extending\"; س م و B004 overhead sky in 91:5. Structural cue: sky/built is followed immediately by earth/spread. Assigned role: earth receives the horizontal expansion corresponding to sky's vertical cover. Abductive move: I infer that the two ayat form a coordinated spatial frame for the later soul; the packet supplies opposite sky/earth and spreading. Reading change: earth is not isolated geography; it is the extended lower plane inside a cosmic enclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_001175/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6aa756e6516039508f70","text":"2. **A cultivation model for self-purification.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء ر ض/B002` is soft fertile ground; 91:9 activates `ف ل ح/B003`, the tiller who splits ground, and `ز ك و/B001`, growth and increase. **Structural cue and role:** spread earth is followed by a shaped self and then by success through `زكاها`, so fertile ground supplies the material role of receiving cultivation and producing increase. **Abductive move:** the agricultural branches are supplied; the arrow \"prepared ground plus cultivation enables growth\" is causal inference. **Reading change:** `طحاها` begins an ethical cultivation mechanism in which the self can be tended into increase.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000637/B001","root_000928/B001","root_001175/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ae838dba00653fc55533","text":"2. **Spread ground as field for cultivation and purification.** Lexical evidence: ء ر ض B002 \"soft fertile ground\"; ط ح و B001 spreading; ف ل ح B003 \"tiller who splits the ground\"; ز ك و B001 \"growth and increase\" in 91:9. Structural cue: spread earth precedes the soul's successful زكّى and failure دسّى. Assigned role: earth becomes the cultivable substrate, while soul purification later becomes a crop-like operation. Abductive move: I infer a material analogy from land cultivation to inner cultivation; the packet supplies fertile ground, tilling, and growth branches. Reading change: the ayah turns into the outer agricultural model for the inner moral work of 91:9.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b3e6e726c4fa88090e55","text":"3. **The same expanse can become a surface of self-burial.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء ر ض/B006` is sticking or sinking toward the ground and fixed `ط ح و/B006` is sprawling against it; 91:10 `د س و/B001` is hidden insertion or concealment. **Structural cue and role:** the paired outcomes of growth in 91:9 and concealment in 91:10 turn the ground from neutral surface into an ambivalent medium: it can host cultivation or receive what is pressed down. **Abductive move:** linking the concealed self to ground-burial is exploratory because 91:10 does not name earth; the sequence and material branch supply, but do not state, that relation. **Reading change:** the spread earth also exposes the risk that made capacity will be used to flatten and hide the self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B002","root_000928/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c7bda7fb5b90b44f4516","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:6:1\",\"root_000025\",[\"B002\"]],[\"91:6:3\",\"root_000928\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yayılan
